    '  onSelectPaziente: (patientId: string) => void;\n' +
    '}\n' +
    'export function MultiPatientParametri({ operatoreNome, onSelectPaziente }: Props) {\n' +
    '  const {\n' +
    '    options: rosterOptions,\n' +
    '    requestKey: rosterKey,\n' +
    '    accept: acceptRoster,\n' +
    '    recover: recoverRoster,\n' +
    '  } = useRosterOrderContext();\n' +
    '  const [draftStore] = useState(createParameterDraftStore);\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => () => draftStore.clear(), [draftStore]);\n' +
    '  const [day, setDay] = useState(() => facilityLocalMinute().slice(0, 10));\n' +
    "  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n" +
    "  // Ultimo elenco gia' mostrato in sessione per giorno/ordine: la pagina compare subito con\n" +
    '  // quello e lo rivalida in background invece di ripartire da "Caricamento pazienti…".\n' +
    '  const initialItems = readSessionCache<PatientParametersPageItem[]>(\n' +
    "    parametersCacheKey('', rosterKey, day),\n" +
    '  );\n' +
    '  const [items, setItems] = useState<PatientParametersPageItem[]>(() => initialItems ?? []);\n' +
    '  const itemsRef = useRef(items);\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => {\n' +
    '    itemsRef.current = items;\n' +
    '  }, [items]);\n' +
    '  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!initialItems);\n' +
    '  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);\n' +
    '  const [summaryLoading, setSummaryLoading] = useState(true);\n' +
    "  const [error, setError] = useState('');\n" +
    '  const [nextCursor, setNextCursor] = useState<string | null>(null);\n' +
    '  const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);\n' +
    '  const generation = useRef(0);\n' +
    '  const moreController = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);\n' +
    '  const currentDay = useRef(day);\n' +
    '  const previousQuery = useRef(query);\n' +
    '  const loadedDay = useRef(day);\n' +
    '  const loadedQuery = useRef<string | null>(null);\n' +
    '  const failedMore = useRef(false);\n' +
    "  const savedSummaries = useRef(new Map<string, SavedParameterReading['summary']>());\n" +
    '  useEffect(() => {\n' +
    '    currentDay.current = day;\n' +
    '  }, [day]);\n' +
    '  const filters = useMemo(\n' +
    '    () => ({\n' +
    '      q: query.trim() || undefined,\n' +
    '      limit: 25,\n' +
    '      date: day,\n' +
    '      month: Number(day.slice(5, 7)),\n' +
    '      year: Number(day.slice(0, 4)),\n' +
    "      view: 'entry' as const,\n" +
    '      ...rosterOptions,\n' +
    '    }),\n' +
    '    [query, day, rosterOptions],\n' +
    '  );\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  const protectSavedSummary = useCallback(\n' +
    '    (item: PatientParametersPageItem): PatientParametersPageItem => {\n' +
    '      item = { ...item, summaryDate: day };\n' +
    '      const saved = savedSummaries.current.get(item.patient.id);\n' +
    '      return saved?.date === day ? applySavedParameterSummary(item, saved) : item;\n' +
    '    },\n' +
    '    [day],\n' +
    '  );\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => {\n' +
    '    const version = ++generation.current;\n' +
    '    const controller = new AbortController();\n' +
    '    moreController.current?.abort();\n' +
    '    let detailedReady = false;\n' +
    '    const hadRows = itemsRef.current.length > 0;\n' +
    '    const load = () => {\n' +
    '      if (controller.signal.aborted) return;\n' +
    "      // Righe gia' a schermo: la rivalidazione non le nasconde dietro il placeholder.\n" +
    '      setLoading(itemsRef.current.length === 0);\n' +
    '      setLoadingMore(false);\n' +
    '      setSummaryLoading(true);\n' +
    '      setNextCursor(null);\n' +
    "      setError('');\n" +
    '      failedMore.current = false;\n' +
    '      if (loadedDay.current !== day) {\n' +
    '        loadedDay.current = day;\n' +
    '        setItems((current) => current.map((item) => resetParameterDay(item, day)));\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      // Identities and daily metadata are independent. The detailed page remains\n' +
    '      // authoritative for membership/cursor, including room searches.\n' +
    '      if (!filters.q) {\n' +
    '        void fetchPatientPage(\n' +
    '          API_URL,\n' +
    '          { limit: 25, ...rosterOptions, asOf: day },\n' +
    '          {\n' +
    '            headers: operatorHeaders(),\n' +
    '            signal: controller.signal,\n' +
    '          },\n' +
    '        )\n' +
    '          .then((page) => {\n' +
    '            if (\n' +
    '              controller.signal.aborted ||\n' +
    '              version !== generation.current ||\n' +
    '              detailedReady ||\n' +
    '              hadRows\n' +
    '            )\n' +
    '              return;\n' +
    '            setItems(\n' +
    '              page.items.map((patient) => ({\n' +
    '                patient,\n' +
    '                cartella: { pazienteId: patient.id, parametriMensili: [] },\n' +
    '                summaryPending: true,\n' +
    '              })),\n' +
    '            );\n' +
    '            setLoading(false);\n' +
    '          })\n' +
    '          .catch(() => {\n' +
    '            /* The parameter page handles failure and can still supply identities. */\n' +
    '          });\n' +
    '      }\n' +
    '      void (async () => {\n' +
    "        const filterKey = JSON.stringify([filters.q ?? '', rosterKey]);\n" +
    '        const sameFilter = loadedQuery.current === filterKey;\n' +
    '        const targetCount = sameFilter ? Math.max(25, itemsRef.current.length) : 25;\n' +
    '        let refreshed: PatientParametersPageItem[] = [];\n' +
    '        let cursor: string | undefined;\n' +
    '        do {\n' +
    '          const page = await fetchPatientParametersPage(\n' +
    '            API_URL,\n' +
    '            { ...filters, cursor },\n' +
    '            {\n' +
    '              headers: operatorHeaders(),\n' +
    '              signal: controller.signal,\n' +
    '            },\n' +
    '          );\n' +
    '          if (controller.signal.aborted || version !== generation.current) return;\n' +
    '          acceptRoster(page.roster);\n' +
    '          detailedReady = true;\n' +
    '          refreshed = mergePatientParametersPage(\n' +
    '            refreshed,\n' +
    '            page.items.map(protectSavedSummary),\n' +
    '            true,\n' +
    '          );\n' +
    '          const needsMore = Boolean(page.nextCursor && refreshed.length < targetCount);\n' +
    '          const snapshot = refreshed;\n' +
    '          // Do not unmount rows on subsequent pages while refreshing their metadata.\n' +
    '          setItems((current) =>\n' +
    '            sameFilter && needsMore\n' +
    '              ? mergePatientParametersPage(current, snapshot, true)\n' +
    '              : snapshot.map(protectSavedSummary),\n' +
    '          );\n' +
    '          setLoading(false);\n' +
    '          setNextCursor(page.nextCursor);\n' +
    '          cursor = needsMore ? page.nextCursor! : undefined;\n' +
    '        } while (cursor);\n' +
    '        loadedQuery.current = filterKey;\n' +
    "        writeSessionCache(parametersCacheKey(filters.q ?? '', rosterKey, day), refreshed);\n" +
    '      })()\n' +
    '        .catch(async (cause) => {\n' +
    '          if (!controller.signal.aborted && version === generation.current) {\n' +
    '            if (isRosterChanged(cause) && (await recoverRoster())) return;\n' +
    '            setError(cause.message);\n' +
    '          }\n' +
    '        })\n' +
    '        .finally(() => {\n' +
    '          if (version === generation.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {\n' +
    '            setLoading(false);\n' +
    '            setSummaryLoading(false);\n' +
    '          }\n' +
    '        });\n' +
    '    };\n' +
    '    const typing = previousQuery.current !== query;\n' +
    '    previousQuery.current = query;\n' +
    '    const timer = window.setTimeout(load, typing ? 250 : 0);\n' +
    '    return () => {\n' +
    '      window.clearTimeout(timer);\n' +
    '      controller.abort();\n' +
    '      moreController.current?.abort();\n' +
    '      generation.current = version + 1;\n' +
    '    };\n' +
    '  }, [\n' +
    '    filters,\n' +
    '    revision,\n' +
    '    rosterKey,\n' +
    '    day,\n' +
    '    query,\n' +
    '    protectSavedSummary,\n' +
    '    rosterOptions,\n' +
    '    acceptRoster,\n' +
    '    recoverRoster,\n' +
    '  ]);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  async function loadMore() {\n' +
    '    if (!nextCursor || moreController.current || loading) return;\n' +
    '    const controller = new AbortController();\n' +
    '    moreController.current = controller;\n' +
    '    const version = generation.current;\n' +
    '    setLoadingMore(true);\n' +

    '    try {\n' +
    '      const page = await fetchPatientParametersPage(\n' +

    '      );\n' +
    '      if (version !== generation.current) return;\n' +
    '      acceptRoster(page.roster);\n' +
    '      setItems((current) =>\n' +
    '        mergePatientParametersPage(current, page.items.map(protectSavedSummary), true),\n' +
    '      );\n' +
    '      setNextCursor(page.nextCursor);\n' +
    '      failedMore.current = false;\n' +
    '    } catch (cause) {\n' +
    '      if (!controller.signal.aborted && version === generation.current) {\n' +
    '        if (isRosterChanged(cause) && (await recoverRoster())) return;\n' +
    '        failedMore.current = true;\n' +
    "        setError('Impossibile caricare altri pazienti. Riprova.');\n" +
    '      }\n' +
    '    } finally {\n' +

    '      const known = savedSummaries.current.get(patientId);\n' +

    '        savedSummaries.current.set(patientId, summary);\n' +
    '      setItems((current) =>\n' +
    '        current.map((item) =>\n' +
    '          item.patient.id !== patientId\n' +
    '            ? item\n' +
    '            : applySavedParameterSummary(item, savedSummaries.current.get(patientId) ?? summary),\n' +
    '        ),\n' +
    '      );\n' +
    '    }\n' +

    '  );\n' +

    '    const load = () => {\n' +
    '      if (controller.signal.aborted) return;\n' +

    '      setSummaryLoading(true);\n' +

    '      // Identities and daily metadata are independent. The detailed page remains\n' +

    '            /* The parameter page handles failure and can still supply identities. */\n' +

    '          }\n' +

    '      setSummaryLoading(true);\n' +
    '      setNextCursor(null);\n' +

    '        loadedDay.current = day;\n' +
    '        setItems((current) => current.map((item) => resetParameterDay(item, day)));\n' +

    '      // Identities and daily metadata are independent. The detailed page remains\n' +
    '      // authoritative for membership/cursor, including room searches.\n' +
    '      if (!filters.q) {\n' +

    '          API_URL,\n' +

    '          {\n' +
    '            headers: operatorHeaders(),\n' +
    '            signal: controller.signal,\n' +
    '          },\n' +
    '        )\n' +

    '              signal: controller.signal,\n' +
